# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Compact Greek 1970 national programmes.
COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_constitution = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_constitution = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_constitution }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_constitution
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_entrench = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_entrench = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_entrench }
		}
		army_experience = 25
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_entrench
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_handover = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_handover = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_handover }
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_POL_handover = yes
		add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_handover days = 180 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_handover
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_development = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_development = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_development }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.01 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_development
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_visitor_credit = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_visitor_credit = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_visitor_credit }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.1 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		random_owned_controlled_state = { # IMPERIA COLD WAR — MD fallback build target
			prioritize = { 143 }
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = offices level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_visitor_credit
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}

		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_gre_treaty = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_gre_treaty }
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_MIL_gre_treaty_apply = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_offer_cyprus_defence = yes
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_gre_treaty
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_gre_escalate = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_gre_escalate = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_gre_escalate }
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_MIL_gre_escalate_apply = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = TUR
				NOT = { has_war_with = CYP }
				CYP = { has_war_with = TUR }
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_greek_direct_entry_used
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_operational_initiative }
				remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_operational_initiative
			}
			add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_gre_escalate days = 180 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_war_with = TUR
				NOT = { has_war_with = CYP }
				CYP = { has_war_with = TUR }
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_greek_direct_entry_used
			}
			set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_gre_escalate
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_staff_network = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_staff_network = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_staff_network }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.015 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		CYP = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_CYP_R2_officer_penetration = 10 }
		}
		CYP = {
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_officer_penetration min = 0 max = 100 }
		}
		add_timed_idea = { idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_staff_network days = 180 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_preparation_active
		country_event = { id = COLDWAR_GRE_R3EXP.1 days = 180 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_staff_network
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}

		random_owned_controlled_state = { # IMPERIA COLD WAR — MD fallback build target
			prioritize = { 142 }

		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_ppc_lignite

COLDWAR_GRE_R3_apply_ppc_hydro = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_can_ppc_hydro = yes
			NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_ppc_hydro }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = gdp_total }
		multiply_temp_variable = { treasury_change = -0.16 }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		# IMPERIA COLD WAR — state-only reward applies while the state is held
		if = {
			limit = { 140 = { is_owned_by = ROOT is_controlled_by = ROOT } }
			140 = {
				set_temp_variable = { electric_addition = 0.9 }
				set_temp_variable = { storage_addition = 0 }
				add_hydroelectric_energy_production_effect = yes
			}
		}
		random_owned_controlled_state = { # IMPERIA COLD WAR — MD fallback build target
			prioritize = { 140 }
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_ppc_hydro
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_GRE_R3_clear_preparation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_preparation_active }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_preparation_active
		CYP = {
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_CYP_R2_officer_penetration = -10 }
			clamp_variable = { var = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_officer_penetration min = 0 max = 100 }
		}
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = {
	if = {
		limit = { original_tag = GRE IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes exists = yes }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3AG_reconcile = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_POL_sync = yes
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_foreign_influence_defense_modifier = 0 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_police_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_political_power_factor = 0 }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_stability_factor = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_constitution }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_political_power_factor = 0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_entrench nationalist_military_junta_are_in_power = yes }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_political_power_factor = 0.15 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_stability_factor = -0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_police_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_handover }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_stability_factor = 0.08 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_tribunals nationalist_military_junta_are_in_power = yes }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_foreign_influence_defense_modifier = 0.1 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_political_power_factor = 0.05 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_police_cost_multiplier_modifier = 0.1 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_stability_factor = -0.03 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_amnesty }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_value_constitutional_administration_political_power_factor = -0.05 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_constitution }
					AND = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_entrench nationalist_military_junta_are_in_power = yes }
					AND = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_handover }

					NOT = { country_exists = CYP }
					NOT = {
						CYP = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_greek_network_active }
					}
					CYP = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_coup_resolved }
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_staff_network
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_operational_initiative
				OR = {
					NOT = { nationalist_military_junta_are_in_power = yes }
					NOT = { country_exists = CYP }
					NOT = {
						CYP = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_CYP_R2_coup_government }
					}
					has_war_with = CYP
					has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_gre_escalate
				}
			}
			remove_ideas = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_operational_initiative
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_preparation_active
				NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_staff_network }
			}
			COLDWAR_GRE_R3_clear_preparation = yes
		}
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_MIL_sync = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3_calculate_expense = {
	set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { original_tag = GRE IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_initialized }
		set_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_army_school has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_army_school }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.00004 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_aegean_command has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_aegean_command }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.00006 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_professional_cadres }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.0001 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_sea_control }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.00015 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_close_support }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.0001 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_external_room }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.00003 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_aegean_deterrence }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.0001 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_security_budget
				nationalist_military_junta_are_in_power = yes
				has_country_leader = { name = "Dimitrios Ioannidis" ruling_only = yes }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.0001 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_staff_network
				nationalist_military_junta_are_in_power = yes
				has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_staff_network
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.0001 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_done_operational_initiative
				nationalist_military_junta_are_in_power = yes
				has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_operational_initiative
			}
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.0003 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_temp_gre_escalate has_war_with = TUR }
			set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = gdp_total }
			multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream = 0.0005 }
			add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_stream }
		}
		add_to_variable = { additional_expenses_rate = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_weekly_expense }
	}
}

		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_GRE_R3_initialized
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}
COLDWAR_GRE_R3_monthly = {
	if = {
		limit = { original_tag = GRE exists = yes IMP_is_1970_scenario = yes }
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_initialize = yes
		COLDWAR_GRE_R3_reconcile = yes
	}
}
